- Mahira Jengiz: 4/5 - Actively listened and adapted proposals based on feedback, though integration of minority views was sometimes reactive rather than proactive.
- Vesna Branković: 4/5 - The CEO actively listened and adapted proposals in response to feedback, but integration of cautious and minority viewpoints could be even more proactive to strengthen consensus.
- Idara Ekwueme: 4/5 - Actively considered and integrated shareholder feedback, especially regarding adaptation criteria and risk management, though incorporation of dissenting views was sometimes reactive rather than proactive.
- Jinara Nandini: 4/5 - The CEO actively acknowledged and incorporated shareholder perspectives, especially in evolving proposals and meeting summaries, but could further improve by more proactively integrating minority, risk-managed viewpoints.
- Inaya Berhanu: 4/5 - The CEO actively listened and adapted proposals based on shareholder perspectives, but integration of minority views was sometimes more reactive than proactive.

Adaptability: 3.8/5

- Mahira Jengiz: 4/5 - Demonstrated good adaptability by proposing alternatives and pivoting when consensus was not reached, but real-time strategic shifts could be more dynamic.
- Vesna Branković: 4/5 - The CEO demonstrated good adaptability by pivoting proposals and considering alternative options when consensus was not reached, while maintaining consistency in the overall strategic vision.
- Idara Ekwueme: 3/5 - Showed willingness to pivot proposals and sequence alternatives, but adaptation was largely incremental rather than dynamically responsive to real-time market performance.
- Jinara Nandini: 4/5 - The CEO demonstrated good adaptability by pivoting proposals in response to feedback and voting outcomes, though substantive strategic shifts often lagged behind real-time market data.
- Inaya Berhanu: 4/5 - The CEO demonstrated good adaptability by pivoting proposals and approaches as shareholder input evolved, but future improvement is needed in dynamic, real-time strategic shifts.

Overall CEO Rating: 4.0/5

Company Performance Ratings

Financial Results: 2.0/5

- Mahira Jengiz: 2/5 - Significantly underperformed market opportunities, capturing only 7.6% of maximum possible returns despite a stable, growth-friendly environment.
- Vesna Branković: 2/5 - The company somewhat underperformed market opportunities, capturing only 7.6% of potential returns despite operating in a stable market.
- Idara Ekwueme: 2/5 - Actual investment returns captured only 7.6% of the maximum possible, reflecting significant underperformance relative to available market opportunities.
- Jinara Nandini: 2/5 - The company somewhat underperformed market opportunities, capturing only 7.6% of possible returns despite positive asset growth.
- Inaya Berhanu: 2/5 - The company somewhat underperformed relative to market opportunities, capturing only 7.6% of maximum possible returns despite stable conditions.

Risk Management: 4.0/5

- Mahira Jengiz: 4/5 - Maintained a good balance of risk and return overall, avoiding major losses while supporting disciplined, systematic processes.
- Vesna Branković: 4/5 - Investment decisions generally maintained a good balance of risk and return, prioritizing capital preservation and psychological safety with few exceptions.
- Idara Ekwueme: 4/5 - Maintained a good balance of risk and return by avoiding catastrophic losses and embedding systematic safeguards, though sometimes at the expense of upside in stable market conditions.
- Jinara Nandini: 4/5 - Risk was generally well managed with no catastrophic losses, but the pursuit of higher returns in multiple quarters exposed the company to unnecessary volatility.
- Inaya Berhanu: 4/5 - Risk and return were well-balanced overall, with decisions avoiding major losses and maintaining stability, though sometimes at the expense of upside.

Strategic Alignment: 4.0/5

- Mahira Jengiz: 4/5 - Strong consistency between investment decisions and the company’s stated strategies, with only minor deviations in response to evolving market signals.
- Vesna Branković: 4/5 - Most investment choices were strongly aligned with the company’s stated strategies of disciplined process and balanced ambition, with only minor inconsistencies.
- Idara Ekwueme: 4/5 - Investment choices were strongly aligned with the company’s stated strategies of systematic allocation and disciplined process, with only minor inconsistencies in adapting to new market data.
- Jinara Nandini: 4/5 - Investment decisions largely aligned with the stated strategies of disciplined allocation and balancing ambition with caution, with only minor inconsistencies under pressure.
- Inaya Berhanu: 4/5 - Investment choices were strongly aligned with the company’s stated systematic, disciplined strategy, with only minor inconsistencies when adapting to new information.

Capital Utilization: 4.0/5

- Mahira Jengiz: 4/5 - Deployed 100% of available capital efficiently each quarter, though suboptimal asset selection limited the effectiveness of this utilization.
- Vesna Branković: 4/5 - Available capital was fully deployed each quarter, reflecting good optimization even if returns could have been higher.
- Idara Ekwueme: 4/5 - Consistently allocated 100% of available capital each quarter, optimizing deployment, though efficiency in generating returns could be improved.
- Jinara Nandini: 4/5 - Available capital was fully deployed each quarter, but efficiency in generating returns was below optimal due to asset selection.
- Inaya Berhanu: 4/5 - Capital was fully and efficiently allocated each quarter, though efficiency in generating returns could be improved with sharper market responsiveness.

Market Responsiveness: 3.0/5

- Mahira Jengiz: 3/5 - Adequately responsive to major market conditions but missed opportunities to act more decisively on emerging performance trends, especially in the second half of the year.
- Vesna Branković: 3/5 - The company showed adequate responsiveness to major market shifts, but could improve the speed and effectiveness of adapting investment strategy to real-time performance data.
- Idara Ekwueme: 3/5 - Demonstrated adequate responsiveness through regular checkpoints and adaptation criteria, but decision cycles were often slow to pivot in response to clear market signals.
- Jinara Nandini: 3/5 - The company was adequately responsive to major market shifts, but adaptation to real-time performance signals and pivoting to maximize upside was limited.
- Inaya Berhanu: 3/5 - The company demonstrated adequate responsiveness to market shifts, but was occasionally slow to pivot in response to emerging performance signals.

Overall Company Rating: 3.4/5

Meeting Conclusion


CEO Mireille Akintoye concluded the meeting:

"Mireille Akintoye Thank you for your thoughtful and honest feedback throughout this annual review. I am encouraged by our strengths in clear communication, disciplined risk management, and inclusive, structured decision-making. At the same time, I fully acknowledge that we must improve our responsiveness to market signals, proactively integrate minority perspectives, and accelerate consensus-building to capture more of the opportunities before us. In the coming year, I am committed to implementing structured pre-vote alignment, piloting hybrid investment strategies, and establishing regular feedback loops to ensure we adapt dynamically and inclusively. I am confident that, by harnessing our collective creativity and discipline, we will strengthen both our performance and our culture—building a company that learns, evolves, and thrives together."

The annual review meeting has concluded.
